JJCAM-CTZOGORIGINAL POSTMay 17 2009, 01:14 PM
Based off of Channel28's Movie Screenshot quiz, I thought that this would be a bit more fitting for the more hardcore gamers of this site. So here's how it's gonna work, I will post a screenshot of a game and based on how obscure the game is, it will be worth more GP. Some will only be worth 10 GP, some 30 or 50, some even 100.Also, members can submit screenshots to me and if I use them in this quiz, that member will get 20 to 50GP (obviously you won't be able to answer when I post your screenshot). Keep in mind that it has to be a screenshot of a recognizable part of the game, yet not be too obvious (such as the title screen).Get enough questions right and you just might get a special surprise or two...:(Anyways, here's #1. How much GP is it worth? You won't know until someone gets it right! ;) -imagehost effed up, answered anyway-

Secondary questions are worth two.5. If a game is said to be multi-platform, a bonus point will be awarded if you guess the console correctly allow with a correct game answer.6. If you change your display name, PM me or post it along with your next guess and I'll update the scoreboard.7. Games may be repeated.8. I am not infallible. If I mess something up, let me know and I'll fix it.

<input type="button" value="Show Spoiler!" style="width:100px;font-size:12px;margin:10px;padding:0px;" onclick="if (this.parentNode.parentNode.getElementsByTagName('div')['show'].style.display != '') { this.parentNode.parentNode.getElementsByTagName('div')['show'].style.display = ''; this.parentNode.parentNode.getElementsByTagName('div')['hide'].style.display = 'none'; this.innerText = ''; this.value = 'Hide Spoiler!'; } else { this.parentNode.parentNode.getElementsByTagName('div')['show'].style.display = 'none'; this.parentNode.parentNode.getElementsByTagName('div')['hide'].style.display = ''; this.innerText = ''; this.value = 'Show Spoiler!'; }" />
1. Obey the forum's posting rules.2. One answer per person per question.3. If a question goes unanswered for a certain amount of time or guesses (at my discretion) I will post a new question with the old one as a secondary question.4. Questions are worth one point. Secondary questions are worth two.5. If a game is said to be multi-platform, a bonus point will be awarded if you guess the console correctly along with a correct game answer.6. If you change your display name, PM me or post it along with your next guess and I'll update the scoreboard.7. Games may be repeated.8. As I live in North America, the screens will be of a North American release unless it is unavailable in North America or unless otherwise specified.9. Spelling is at my discretion. If I feel it is obvious that what you typed was intended to be what the correct answer is, points will be rewarded. If I have no idea if it was even in English, then you need to take a typing class.10. I do not include re-releases on WiiWare (Virtual Console), PSN, Steam, etc. as a separate console. For example, Final Fantasy: Mystic Quest was re-released as a download on Wii, but I still consider it to not be a multi-platform game. Full reboots, such as Final Fantasy 3 on DS, do not count either unless otherwise specified.11. I am not infallible. If I mess something up, let me know and I'll fix it.

1. Obey the forum's posting rules.2. One answer per person per question.3. If a question goes unanswered for a certain amount of time or guesses (at my discretion) I will post a new question with the old one as a secondary question.4. Questions are worth one point. Secondary questions are worth two.5. If a game is said to be multi-platform, a bonus point will be awarded if you guess the console correctly along with a correct game answer.6. If you change your display name, PM me or post it along with your next guess and I'll update the scoreboard.7. Games may be repeated.8. As I live in North America, the screens will be of a North American release unless it is unavailable in North America or unless otherwise specified.9. Spelling is at my discretion. If I feel it is obvious that what you typed was intended to be what the correct answer is, points will be rewarded. If I have no idea if it was even in English, then you need to take a typing class.10. I do not include re-releases on WiiWare (Virtual Console), PSN, Steam, etc. as a separate console. For example, Final Fantasy: Mystic Quest was re-released as a download on Wii, but I still consider it to not be a multi-platform game. Full reboots, such as Final Fantasy 3 on DS, do not count either unless otherwise specified.11. When answering, you must specify which question you are answering with its number. For Example: Q1: The answer is Super Mario Bros. for NES.12. I am not infallible. If I mess something up, let me know and I'll fix it.

1. Obey the forum's posting rules.2. One answer per person per question.3. If a question goes unanswered for a certain amount of time or guesses (at my discretion) I will post a new question with the old one as a secondary question.4. Questions are worth one point. Secondary questions are worth two.5. If a game is said to be multi-platform, a bonus point will be awarded if you guess the console correctly along with a correct game answer.6. If you change your display name, PM me or post it along with your next guess and I'll update the scoreboard.7. Games may be repeated.8. As I live in North America, the screens will be of a North American release unless it is unavailable in North America or unless otherwise specified.9. Spelling is at my discretion. If I feel it is obvious that what you typed was intended to be what the correct answer is, points will be rewarded. If I have no idea if it was even in English, then you need to take a typing class.10. I do not include re-releases on WiiWare (Virtual Console), PSN, Steam, etc. as a separate console. For example, Final Fantasy: Mystic Quest was re-released as a download on Wii, but I still consider it to not be a multi-platform game. Full reboots, such as Final Fantasy 3 on DS, do not count either unless otherwise specified.11. When answering, you must specify which question you are answering with its number. For Example: Q1: The answer is Super Mario Bros. for NES.12. I am not infallible. If I mess something up, let me know and I'll fix it.

1. Obey the forum's posting rules.2. One answer per person per question.3. If a question goes unanswered for a certain amount of time or guesses (at my discretion) I will post a new question with the old one in an EXTRA QUESTIONS section.4. Questions are worth one point. Extra Questions vary.5. If a game is said to be multi-platform, a bonus point will be awarded if you guess the console correctly along with a correct game answer.6. If you change your display name, PM me or include a note in your post along with your next guess and I'll update the scoreboard.7. Games may be repeated.8. As I live in North America, the screens will be of a North American release unless it is unavailable in North America or unless otherwise specified.9. Spelling is at my discretion. If I feel it is obvious that what you typed was intended to be what the correct answer is, points will be rewarded. If I have no idea if it was even in English, then you need to take a typing class.10. I do not include re-releases on WiiWare (Virtual Console), PSN, Steam, etc. as a separate console. For example, Final Fantasy: Mystic Quest was re-released as a download on Wii, but I still consider it to not be a multi-platform game. Full reboots, such as Final Fantasy 3 on DS, do not count either unless otherwise specified. Arcade is a "console".11. When answering, you must specify which question you are answering with its number. For Example: Q1: The answer is Super Mario Bros. for NES.12. I am not infallible. If I mess something up, let me know and I'll fix it.

If you get the game title right, then I tell you that you were wrong on the system guess, you are allowed to offer another system guess, once for each time I correct you and only for the system if you have properly guessed the title.3. If a question goes unanswered for a certain amount of time or guesses (at my discretion) I will post a new question with the old one in an EXTRA QUESTIONS section.4. Questions are worth one point. Extra Questions vary.5. If a game is said to be multi-platform, a bonus point will be awarded if you guess the console correctly along with a correct game answer.6. If you change your display name, PM me or include a note in your post along with your next guess and I'll update the scoreboard.7. Games may be repeated.8. As I live in North America, the screens will be of a North American release unless it is unavailable in North America or unless otherwise specified.9. Spelling is at my discretion. If I feel it is obvious that what you typed was intended to be what the correct answer is, points will be rewarded. If I have no idea if it was even in English, then you need to take a typing class.10. I do not include re-releases on WiiWare (Virtual Console), PSN, Steam, etc. as a separate console. For example, Final Fantasy: Mystic Quest was re-released as a download on Wii, but I still consider it to not be a multi-platform game. Full reboots, such as Final Fantasy 3 on DS, do not count either unless otherwise specified. Arcade is a "console". I also do not count PC/Mac/Linux as separate cosoles.11. When answering, you must specify which question you are answering with its number. For Example: Q1: The answer is Super Mario Bros. for NES.12. I am not infallible. If I mess something up, let me know and I'll fix it.
